\Delta_{1}^{(-i)}=\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)\top}_{r}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r})^{-1}-\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0})^{-1}
and the
t
-th row of it as
\Delta_{1,t}^{(-i)\top}
. Then, because
(\epsilon_{it})_{t\in[T]}
are independent of
\Delta_{1}^{(-i)}
by construction, we have by Assumption B (or B’) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{1}^{(-i)}\right\|^{2}\right|\Delta_{1}^{(-i)},\bm{M}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\epsilon_{it}\Delta_{1,t}^{(-i)}\right\|^{2}\right|\Delta_{1}^{(-i)},\bm{M}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{r}|\Delta^{(-i)\top}_{1,l}\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{i})\Delta^{(-i)}_{1,l}|\leq\sum_{l=1}^{r}\left\|\Delta^{(-i)}_{1,l}\right\|^{2}\left\|\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{i})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{i})\right\|\left\|\Delta_{1}^{(-i)}\right\|_{F}^{2}\lesssim\left\|\Delta_{1}^{(-i)}\right\|_{F}^{2}

(7)

where
\mathbf{e}_{i}=[\epsilon_{i1},\dots,\epsilon_{iT}]^{\top}
. By the perturbation theory for pseudo-inverses (e.g., Lemma 12 of
Chen et al., 2019
) with Lemma
B.3
(ii), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\Delta_{1}^{(-i)}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\max\left\{\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)\top}_{r}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r})^{-1}\right\|^{2},\left\|\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0})^{-1}\right\|^{2}\right\}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r}-\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\psi_{\min}\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)

since
\max\{||\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)\top}_{r}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r})^{-1}||^{2},||\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0})^{-1}||^{2}\}=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\psi_{\min}}\right)
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{1}^{(-i)}\right\|^{2}\right|\Delta_{1}^{(-i)},\bm{M}^{0}\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{r\kappa^{4}\max\{N,T\}}{\psi_{\min}^{3}}\right),

and by Lemma 6.1 of
Chernozhukov et al., 2018
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)\top}_{r}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r})^{-1}-\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0})^{-1}\right)\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{r^{1/2}\kappa^{2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\psi_{\min}\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right).

Next, we bound the second term. Define
\Delta_{2}^{(-i)}=\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}_{r}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{-1}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)\top}_{r}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{(-i)}_{r})^{-1}
. By the the perturbation bound for pseudo-inverses with Lemma
B.3
(iv), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{2}^{(-i)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{E}\right\|\left\|\Delta_{2}^{(-i)}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{3}\rho_{i}\sqrt{T}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\psi_{\min}^{3/2}}+\frac{\kappa^{3}r^{1/2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\psi_{\min}^{3/2}}\right).

Proof of Lemma
B.2
.
First, note that

\displaystyle\left\|R_{2,i}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|Y_{i}^{0\top}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{-1}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{-1}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|Y_{i}^{0}\right\|\left\|(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}\right\|\left\|(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{1/2}\rho_{i}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)\left\|(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}\right\|

since
||(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{-1}||=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\psi_{\min}}\right)
and
||u_{i}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(\rho_{i}\right)
. Let
(\Delta_{y},\Delta_{z})=(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}-\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0})
. Note that
(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}=\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}+\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z}
. Using the decomposition of
\Delta_{z}
and Claim
1
, we get

\displaystyle\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left[\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}(\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}-I_{r})+\bm{E}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}\right]^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}

\displaystyle=-(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\Delta_{y}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0}\underbrace{-(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0}+(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}}_{\coloneqq P}

\displaystyle=-(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}\left(\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\frac{1}{2}(\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}-\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z})\right)\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0}+P,

and

(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}+\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0}=(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})P+\frac{1}{2}(\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}-\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z})\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0}
.

Claim 1
.

\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\Delta_{y}=\frac{1}{2}(\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}-\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z}).

Then, applying the Sylvester equation (e.g., Lemma 15 of
Chen et al., 2019
), we have with probability converging to
1
that

\displaystyle\left\|\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\psi_{\min}}\left\|(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})P+\frac{1}{2}(\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}-\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z})\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\psi_{\min}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0}+\frac{1}{2}(\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}-\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z})\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\psi_{\min}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\frac{1}{2}(\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z}+\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y})\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0}\right\|,

and

\displaystyle\left\|(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}\right\|\leq\left\|\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\right\|+\left\|\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z}\right\|\leq\frac{1}{\psi_{\min}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\right\|+\kappa\left(\left\|\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z}\right\|+\left\|\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}\right\|\right).

First, from Lemma
B.3
(ii), we know
\kappa(||\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z}||+||\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}||)=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{5}\max\{N,T\}}{\psi_{\min}}\right)
. Then, it is enough to bound
||\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}||
. Note that
||\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}||\leq||\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}||+||\Delta_{y}^{\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}||
. In addition, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right|\bm{M}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{l=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\epsilon_{it}Y_{i,k}^{0}Z_{t,l}^{0}\right)^{2}\right|\bm{M}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\left(Y_{i,k}^{0}\right)^{2}\right)\sum_{l=1}^{r}\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\epsilon_{it}\epsilon_{is}\right|\bm{M}^{0}\right]Z_{t,l}^{0}Z_{s,l}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}O_{p}\left(r\kappa\psi_{\min}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(r^{2}\kappa^{2}\psi_{\min}^{2}\right)

since
||\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}||_{F}^{2}\leq r\kappa\psi_{\min}
and for all
l\in[r]
,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\epsilon_{it}\epsilon_{is}\right|\bm{M}^{0}\right]Z_{t,l}^{0}Z_{s,l}^{0}\right|\leq\left|\mathbf{Z}_{r,l}^{0\top}\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{i}|\bm{M}^{0})\mathbf{Z}_{r,l}^{0}\right|\leq\left\|\mathbf{Z}_{r,l}^{0}\right\|^{2}\left\|\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{i})\right\|=O_{p}(\kappa\psi_{\min}).

So, we have
||\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(r\kappa\psi_{\min}\right)
. In addition,

\left\|\Delta_{y}^{\top}\bm{E}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}\right\|\leq\left\|\Delta_{y}\right\|\left\|\bm{E}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\kappa^{5/2}\max\{N,T\}\right).

Hence,
||\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\bm{E}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(r\kappa\psi_{\min}+\kappa^{5/2}\max\{N,T\}\right)
,
||(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}||=O_{p}\left(r\kappa+\frac{\kappa^{5}\max\{N,T\}}{\psi_{\min}}\right)
, and

\left\|R_{2,i}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{11/2}\rho_{i}\max\{N,T\}}{\psi_{\min}^{3/2}}+\frac{r\kappa^{3/2}\rho_{i}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right).

Proof of Claim
1
.

Since
\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}=\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}
,
\bm{Y}_{r}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{r}=\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}
, and
\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}=\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\Delta_{y}

\displaystyle=(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}-\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0})^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0})

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r})+\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}-\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}-(\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}-\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{\prime}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{y}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}+\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}-\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0\top}\Delta_{z}-\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z}.

(8)

In addition, by Lemma 35 of
Ma et al., 2020
, we have
\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}+\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}_{r}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\succeq 0
and it implies that
\Delta_{y}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}+\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}
is a symmetric matrix. Then, since
\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\Delta_{y}=\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0\top}\Delta_{z}-\Delta_{y}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}
, we have
\Delta_{z}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\Delta_{y}=\frac{1}{2}(\Delta_{y}^{\top}\Delta_{y}-\Delta_{z}^{\top}\Delta_{z})
from (
B.1
).
\square

Technical Lemmas.

First, we introduce several notations. Let
\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}^{(-i)}_{r}\\
\mathbf{Z}^{(-i)}_{r}\end{bmatrix}
,

\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}\\
\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}
,

\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-i)}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-i)}_{r}\end{bmatrix}
, and

\mathbf{W}_{r}^{0}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}_{r}^{0}\\
\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}
. We define
\bar{\bm{X}}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{X}\\
\bm{X}^{\top}&0\end{bmatrix}
,
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{X}^{(-i)}\\
\bm{X}^{(-i)\top}&0\end{bmatrix}
, and
\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{M}^{0}\\
\bm{M}^{0\top}&0\end{bmatrix}
. In addition, the rotation matrices related to the leave-one-out estimator are defined as

\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}=\argmin_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}^{(-i)}_{r}\\
\mathbf{Z}^{(-i)}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}-\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}\\
\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}
,
\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}=\argmin_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-i)}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-i)}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}-\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}_{r}^{0}\\
\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}
,

\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}=\argmin_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-i)}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-i)}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}-\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}
,

\mathbf{R}^{(-i)}=\argmin_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}_{r}\\
\mathbf{Z}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{O}-\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}^{(-i)}_{r}\\
\mathbf{Z}^{(-i)}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}\right\|_{F}
.

Lemma B.3
.

We have

(i)
||\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}||,||\bar{\bm{X}}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}\right)=o_{p}\left(\psi_{\min}\right)
;

(ii)
||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}||,||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)
;

(iii)
||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{R}^{(-i)}||_{F}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{T}\rho_{i}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}+\frac{\kappa\sqrt{r}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)
;

(iv)
||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}||_{F}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{3}\sqrt{T}\rho_{i}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{r}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)
.

Proof of Lemma
B.3
.
(i) Because
\bm{X}^{(-i)}-\bm{M}^{0}=\bm{E}^{(-i)}
where
\bm{E}^{(-i)}
replaces the
i
-th row of
\bm{E}
with zeros, we can easily know that
||\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}||=||\bm{X}^{(-i)}-\bm{M}^{0}||\leq||\bm{E}||=O_{p}\left(\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}\right)
. Similarly, we have
||\bar{\bm{X}}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}\right)
.

(ii) Consider the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)})^{1/2}(\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)})+\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}((\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)})^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2})\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{0})(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)1/2}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}\right\|+\left\|\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\right\|+\left\|(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{0}\right\|.

Note that
\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)\top}
is the top-
r
eigenvalue decomposition of
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}
. So, by Weyl’s inequality with the result (i), we have with probability converging to 1,

\frac{3}{4}\psi_{r}(\bm{M}^{0})\leq\psi_{r}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)})\leq\psi_{1}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)})\leq 2\psi_{1}(\bm{M}^{0}).

Then, by Lemmas B.2, B.3, and B.4 of
Chen et al., 2020a
,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{3/2}}{\psi_{\min}}\right)\left\|\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{3/2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\psi_{\min}}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)})^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)\left\|\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{Q}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{0}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\psi_{\min}}\right)\left\|\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\psi_{\min}}\right).

Hence, we can get
||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)
. By the same token, we can get the result for
||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}||
.

(iii) By definition, we have

||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{R}^{(-i)}||_{F}\leq||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}||_{F}
. Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}((\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)})^{1/2}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2})\right\|_{F}+\left\|(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{W}_{r})\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)})^{1/2}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{W}_{r}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}\right\|.

By Lemma B.3 of
Chen et al., 2020a
, we have

\left\|\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i)1/2}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\right\|_{F}.

In addition, by Davis-Kahan theorem, we have

\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\mathbf{W}_{r}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa}{\psi_{\min}}\right)\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\right\|_{F}.

So, we have

\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\right\|_{F}.

Note that

\displaystyle(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{E}^{(i)}\\
\bm{E}^{(i)\top}&0\end{bmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-i)}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-i)}_{r}\end{bmatrix},\ \ \text{ where }\bm{E}^{(i)}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
\epsilon_{i1}&\cdots&\epsilon_{iT}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
0&\cdots&0\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\left[(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\right]^{\top}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}0&\cdots&0&\sum_{t=1}^{T}\epsilon_{it}v_{t}^{(-i)}&0&\cdots&0&\epsilon_{i1}u_{i}^{(-i)}&\cdots&\epsilon_{iT}u_{i}^{(-i)}\end{bmatrix}

where
u_{i}^{(-i)\top}
and
v_{t}^{(-i)\top}
are the
i
-th row of
\mathbf{U}_{r}^{(-i)}
and the
t
-th row of
\mathbf{V}_{r}^{(-i)}
, respectively. Hence, we have

\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\right\|_{F}\lesssim\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\epsilon_{it}v_{t}^{(-i)}\right\|+\left\|[\epsilon_{i1}u_{i}^{(-i)},\cdots,\epsilon_{iT}u_{i}^{(-i)}]\right\|_{F}.

By using the same assertion in (
B.1
), the first term is bounded like

\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\epsilon_{it}v_{t}^{(-i)}\right\|^{2}\right|\mathbf{V}_{r}^{(-i)},\bm{M}^{0}\right]\lesssim||\mathbf{V}_{r}^{(-i)}||_{F}^{2}=r.

In addition, by Lemma
B.4
, the second term is bounded like

\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|[\epsilon_{i1}u_{i}^{(-i)},\cdots,\epsilon_{iT}u_{i}^{(-i)}]\right\|_{F}^{2}\right|\mathbf{U}_{r}^{(-i)},\bm{M}^{0}\right]=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}^{2}\right]\left\|u_{i}^{(-i)}\right\|^{2}\lesssim T\left\|u_{i}^{(-i)}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\kappa^{2}T\left\|u_{i}^{0}\right\|^{2}\right).

Then, we have
||(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}||_{F}=O_{p}\left(\kappa\sqrt{T}\rho_{i}+\sqrt{r}\right)
and

\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{R}^{(-i)}\right\|_{F}\leq\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{B}^{(-i)}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{T}\rho_{i}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}+\frac{\kappa\sqrt{r}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right).

(iv) By the result in (ii), we have with probability converging to 1,

\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}\right\|\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}\right\|\lesssim r\kappa^{5/2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}\ll\psi_{\min},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{R}^{(-i)}\right\|\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}\right\|\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}\right\|+\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}\right\|\right)\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim r\kappa^{5/2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}

\displaystyle\ll\psi_{\min}.

Hence, by applying Lemma 22 of
Chen et al., 2020b
, we have with probability converging to 1,

\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{O}^{(-i)}\right\|_{F}\leq 5\kappa\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-i)}\mathbf{R}^{(-i)}\right\|_{F}.\ \ \square

Lemma B.4
.

When
\left\|u_{i}^{0}\right\|\ll 1/\kappa
and
\left\|\bm{E}\right\|\ll\psi_{\min}
, we have
||u_{i}^{(-i)}||\lesssim\kappa||u_{i}^{0}||
.

Proof of Lemma
B.4
.
Denote the matrix derived by zeroing out the
i
-th row and column of
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)}
and the corresponding leading
r
eigenvectors by
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i),zero}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{X}^{(-i),zero}\\
\bm{X}^{(-i),zero\top}&0\end{bmatrix}
and
\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-i),zero}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-i),zero}_{r}\end{bmatrix}
, respectively. Here,
\mathbf{U}^{(-i),zero}_{r}\mathbf{D}^{(-i),zero}_{r}\mathbf{V}^{(-i),zero\top}_{r}
is the top-r singular value decomposition of
\bm{X}^{(-i),zero}

where
\bm{X}^{(-i),zero}
is the matrix derived by zeroing out the
i
-th row of
\bm{X}^{(-i)}
. Note that

\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-i),zero}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero\top}
is the top-
r
eigenvalue decomposition of
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i),zero}
.

First, we confirm that
||u^{(-i),zero}_{i}||=0
. Note that entries on
i
-th row of
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i),zero}
is zeros. So, if there is an eigenvector whose entries on
i
-th row is not zero, the corresponding eigenvalue must be zero. However, the top-
r
eigenvalues of
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i),zero}
are bigger than
\frac{1}{2}\psi_{\min}
by Weyl’s theorem because

\left\|\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i),zero}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}\right\|=\left\|\bm{X}^{(-i),zero}-\bm{M}^{0}\right\|\leq\left\|\bm{M}^{0}_{i,\cdot}\right\|+\left\|\bm{E}^{(-i)}\right\|<\frac{1}{4}\psi_{\min}.

It follows from
\left\|\bm{M}^{0}_{i,\cdot}\right\|=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}(m_{it}^{0})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\leq\kappa\psi_{\min}\left\|u_{i}^{0}\right\|<\frac{1}{8}\psi_{\min}
by the assumption
\left\|u_{i}^{0}\right\|\ll 1/\kappa
, and
\left\|\bm{E}^{(-i)}\right\|\leq\left\|\bm{E}\right\|<\frac{1}{8}\psi_{\min}
. Then, since
\mathbf{W}^{(-i),zero}_{r}
is the collection of top-
r
eigenvectors, we have
W^{(-i),zero}_{i}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}u^{(-i),zero}_{i}=0
.

Next, we bound
||\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}||_{F}
. By Davis-Kahan theorem, we have

\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{\psi_{\min}}\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i),zero}-\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}\right\|_{F}.

For
l\neq i
, we have

(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i),zero}-\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)})_{l,\cdot}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}=(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i),zero}-\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)})_{l,i}W^{(-i),zero}_{i}=0

because
W^{(-i),zero}_{i}=0
. So, we have

\displaystyle\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i),zero}-\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-i)})_{i,\cdot}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}\right\|_{2}=\left\|\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}_{i,\cdot}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}_{i,\cdot}\right\|_{2}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}(m_{it}^{0})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\leq\kappa\psi_{\min}\left\|u_{i}^{0}\right\|

and
||\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}||_{F}\leq 2\sqrt{2}\kappa||u_{i}^{0}||
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|u_{i}^{(-i)}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\left\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\left\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\left\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}\right\|+\sqrt{2}\left\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i)\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-i),zero}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\left\|u_{i}^{0}\right\|,

where
e_{i}
is the
i
-th column of the
(N+T)\times(N+T)
identity matrix
I_{(N+T)}
.
\square

B.2
Proof of Proposition
A.2

Proof of Proposition
A.2
(i).

Note that Lemmas
B.1
and
B.2
allow the weak dependence of error terms across time (Assumptions B’(i) – (iii)). Hence, Proposition
A.2
(i) simply follows from Lemmas
B.1
and
B.2
.

Proof of Proposition
A.2
(ii).

By the same token as (
3
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{O}^{\top}Z_{t}-Z^{0}_{t}=(\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0})^{-1}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\mathbf{e}_{t}+\underbrace{\left((\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}-(\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0})^{-1}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}\right)\mathbf{e}_{t}}_{\coloneqq R_{1,t}}+\underbrace{\left((\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{\top}_{r}\bm{Y}^{0}_{r}-I_{r}\right)Z_{t}^{0}}_{\coloneqq R_{2,t}},

where
\mathbf{e}_{t}=[\epsilon_{1t},\dots,\epsilon_{Nt}]
.

Lemma B.5
.

Under the assumption for Proposition
A.2
(ii),

\displaystyle\left\|R_{1,t}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{5/2}\sqrt{N}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}(\log N)^{\nu}q_{2,t}}{\psi_{\min}^{3/2}}+\frac{r^{1/2}\kappa^{5/2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}N^{1/6}}{\psi_{\min}^{3/2}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+\frac{\kappa^{2}N\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}(\log N)^{\nu}q_{3,t}}{\psi_{\min}^{5/2}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{N}\max\{N^{3/2},T^{3/2}\}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi_{\min}^{7/2}}\right).

Lemma B.6
.

Under the assumption for Proposition
A.2
(ii),

\displaystyle\left\|R_{2,t}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{11/2}q_{1,t}\max\{N,T\}}{\psi_{\min}^{3/2}}+\frac{r\kappa^{3/2}q_{1,t}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right).

Proof of Lemma
B.5
.
First, we define the leave-neighbor-out estimator. Denote the neighbor of
t
by
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)=\{t-\delta,\cdots,t,\cdots,t+\delta\}
. Here, we set
\delta=C\lceil(\log N)^{\nu}\rceil
for some large constant
C\geq 0
. Then, we denote by
\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
the matrix that replaces the columns in
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)
of
\bm{X}
with
(m^{0}_{is})_{i\in[N],s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}
to remove the noises
(\epsilon_{is})_{i\in[N],s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}
. The corresponding top-r singular value decomposition is denoted by
\mathbf{U}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{V}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}
and
(\bm{Y}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))},\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})=(\mathbf{U}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{1/2},\mathbf{V}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{1/2})
. In addition, the corresponding rotation matrix is
\mathbf{O}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\argmin_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\\
\mathbf{Z}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}-\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}\\
\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}
. Then, consider the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{R}_{1,t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq||\underbrace{\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\top}\left(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}_{r}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r})^{-1}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}(\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0})^{-1}\right)}_{\coloneqq a_{1}}||

\displaystyle\ \ +||\underbrace{\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\top}\left(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}-\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}_{r}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r})^{-1}\right)}_{\coloneqq a_{2}}||

where
\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}=\bm{Y}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\mathbf{O}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
. We bound
||a_{1}||
. Define

\Delta_{1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}_{r}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r})^{-1}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}(\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0})^{-1}

and the
i
-th row of it as
\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}
. Then, the first term can be represented as

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{it}\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\epsilon_{it}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]\right)\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}

\displaystyle\ \ +\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}]\right)\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}.

Then, because conditioning on
\{(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},\bm{M}^{0}\}
, we can treat
\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
as a constant and
\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},\bm{M}^{0}]=\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\epsilon_{it}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]\right)\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|^{2}\right|(\epsilon_{jt})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},\bm{M}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{r}|\Delta^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}_{1,l}\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{t}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}})\Delta^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{1,l}|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{r}\left\|\Delta^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{1,l}\right\|^{2}\left\|\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{t}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}})\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{t}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}})\right\|\left\|\Delta_{1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(N^{1/3}\right)\left\|\Delta_{1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|_{F}^{2}

by Lemma
B.10
. In addition, by the perturbation theory for pseudo-inverses with Lemma
B.7
(ii), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\Delta_{1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\max\left\{\left\|\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}_{r}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r})^{-1}\right\|^{2},\left\|\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}(\bm{Y}_{r}^{0\top}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0})^{-1}\right\|^{2}\right\}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}-\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\psi_{\min}\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right).

Hence, we have

\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\epsilon_{it}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]\right)\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{r^{1/2}\kappa^{2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}N^{1/6}}{\psi_{\min}\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right).

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}]\right)\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}]\right)\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}]\right)^{2}\right)\left\|\Delta_{1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|_{F}^{2}.

By Assumption B’(iv), we have
\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}]\right)^{2}=O_{p}(N^{1/3})
. Moreover, by Lemma
B.7
(ii), we have

||\Delta_{1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}||_{F}^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{r\kappa^{4}\max\{N,T\}}{\psi_{\min}^{3}}\right)
.
Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{js})_{j\in[N],s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}]\right)\Delta_{1,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{r^{1/2}\kappa^{2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}N^{1/6}}{\psi_{\min}\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right).

(9)

Next, we bound
||a_{2}||
. Define

\Delta_{2}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{\top}_{r}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r})^{-1}-\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}_{r}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r})^{-1}.

By the the perturbation bound for pseudo-inverses with Lemma
B.7
(iv), we have

\displaystyle\left\|a_{2}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\top}\Delta_{2}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{E}\right\|\left\|\Delta_{2}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\psi_{\min}}\right)\left\|\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{r}-\widetilde{\bm{Y}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{3}\sqrt{N}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}(\log N)^{\nu}q_{2,t}}{\psi_{\min}^{3/2}}+\frac{r^{1/2}\kappa^{5/2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi_{\min}^{3/2}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.+\frac{\kappa^{2}N\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}(\log N)^{\nu}q_{3,t}}{\psi_{\min}^{5/2}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{N}\max\{N^{3/2},T^{3/2}\}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi_{\min}^{7/2}}\right).\ \ \square

Proof of Lemma
B.6
.

This proof is same as the proof of Lemma
B.2
. Hence, we omit it.
\square

Technical Lemmas

We introduce several notations. Let
\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\\
\mathbf{Z}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)))}_{r}\end{bmatrix}
,

\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}\\
\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}
,

\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\end{bmatrix}
, and

\mathbf{W}_{r}^{0}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}_{r}^{0}\\
\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}
. We define
\bar{\bm{X}}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{X}\\
\bm{X}^{\top}&0\end{bmatrix}
,
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\\
\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}&0\end{bmatrix}
, and
\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{M}^{0}\\
\bm{M}^{0\top}&0\end{bmatrix}
. In addition, the rotation matrices related to the leave-neighbor-out estimator are defined as

\displaystyle\mathbf{O}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\argmin_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\\
\mathbf{Z}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}-\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}_{r}^{0}\\
\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F},\ \ \mathbf{Q}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\argmin_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}-\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}_{r}^{0}\\
\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F},

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\argmin_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}-\begin{bmatrix}U_{r}\\
V_{r}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F},\ \ \mathbf{R}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\argmin_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}_{r}\\
\mathbf{Z}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{O}-\begin{bmatrix}\bm{Y}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\\
\mathbf{Z}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}\right\|_{F}.

Lemma B.7
.

We have

(i)
||\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}||,||\bar{\bm{X}}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}\right)=o_{p}\left(\psi_{\min}\right)
;

(ii)
||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{O}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}||,||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)
;

\displaystyle(iii)\ \ ||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{R}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}||_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{N}(\log N)^{\nu}q_{2,t}}{\psi^{1/2}_{\min}}+\frac{r^{1/2}\kappa^{3/2}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi^{1/2}_{\min}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.+\frac{\kappa Nq_{3,t}(\log N)^{\nu}}{\psi^{3/2}_{\min}}+\frac{\kappa\sqrt{N}\max\{N,T\}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi_{\min}^{5/2}}\right);

\displaystyle(iv)\ \ ||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{O}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}||_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{3}\sqrt{N}(\log N)^{\nu}q_{2,t}}{\psi^{1/2}_{\min}}+\frac{r^{1/2}\kappa^{5/2}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi^{1/2}_{\min}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.+\frac{\kappa^{2}Nq_{3,t}(\log N)^{\nu}}{\psi^{3/2}_{\min}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{N}\max\{N,T\}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi_{\min}^{5/2}}\right).

Proof of Lemma
B.7
.

(i) Because
\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bm{M}^{0}=\bf\bm{E}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
where
\bf\bm{E}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
replaces the columns in
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)
of
\bm{E}
with zeros, we can easily know that
||\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}||=||\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bm{M}^{0}||\leq||\bm{E}||=O_{p}\left(\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}\right)
. Similarly, we have
||\bar{\bm{X}}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}||=O_{p}\left(\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}\right)
.

(ii) This proof is the same as that of Lemma
B.3
(ii). Hence, we omit it.

(iii) By definition,
||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{R}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}||_{F}\leq||\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}||_{F}
. Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}((\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{1/2}\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2})\right\|_{F}+\left\|(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\mathbf{W}_{r})\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{1/2}\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\mathbf{W}_{r}\right\|_{F}\left\|\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}\right\|.

By Lemma B.3 of
Chen et al., 2020a
, we have

\left\|(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{1/2}\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|_{F}.

In addition, by Davis-Kahan theorem, we have

\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\mathbf{W}_{r}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa}{\psi_{\min}}\right)\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|_{F}.

So, we have

\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{B}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\psi_{\min}}}\right)\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|_{F}.

Note that

\displaystyle(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{E}^{(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\\
\bm{E}^{(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}&0\end{bmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\text{ where }\ \ \bm{E}^{(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\cdots&\epsilon_{1,t-\delta}&\cdots&\epsilon_{1,t+\delta}&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
0&\cdots&\epsilon_{N,t-\delta}&\cdots&\epsilon_{N,t+\delta}&\cdots&0\\
\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\epsilon_{1s}v_{s}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\\
\vdots\\
\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\epsilon_{Ns}v_{s}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\\
0\\
\vdots\\
0\\
\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{i,t-\delta}u_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\\
\vdots\\
\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{i,t+\delta}u_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\\
0\end{bmatrix}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\underbrace{\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{i,t-\delta}u_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))},\cdots,\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{i,t+\delta}u_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right]}_{\coloneqq a_{1}}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\underbrace{\left[\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\epsilon_{1s}v_{s}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))},\cdots,\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\epsilon_{Ns}v_{s}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right]}_{\coloneqq a_{2}}\right\|_{F}.

We bound
||a_{1}||_{F}
first. Using the decomposition
(
B.8
) in Lemma
B.8
, we have

\displaystyle a_{1}=(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{-1}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{i,t-\delta}u_{i}^{0},\cdots,\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{i,t+\delta}u_{i}^{0}\right]\ \ \cdots\cdots(a_{1-1})

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{\top}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}\epsilon_{i,t-\delta}\epsilon_{ik}v_{k}^{0},\cdots,\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}\epsilon_{i,t+\delta}\epsilon_{ik}v_{k}^{0}\right]\ \ \cdots(a_{1-2})

\displaystyle+T^{1/2}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{i,t-\delta}R_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))},\dots,\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{i,t+\delta}R_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right]\ \ \cdots\cdots(a_{1-3})

Note that

\left\|a_{1-1}\right\|_{F}^{2}\leq\left\|(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{-1}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\right\|^{2}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{is}u_{i}^{0}\right\|^{2}.

Because
\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{-1}=O_{p}(1)
and
(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{-1}=O_{p}(\psi_{\min}^{1/2})
by Lemma
C.2
, we have

\left\|(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{-1}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\right\|=O_{p}(\kappa^{1/2}).

Moreover, since
\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{is}u_{i}^{0}\right\|^{2}\right|\bm{M}^{0}\right]\leq||\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{s})||||\mathbf{U}_{r}^{0}||_{F}^{2}\lesssim r
where
|\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)|=2\delta+1\asymp(\log N)^{\nu}
, we have
||a_{1-1}||_{F}^{2}=O_{p}\left(r\kappa(\log N)^{\nu}\right)
. In addition, note that

\left\|a_{1-2}\right\|\leq\left\|(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{\top}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\right\|\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}v_{k}^{0}\right\|.

Because
\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=O_{p}(1)
and
\mathbf{H}^{0}=O_{p}(\psi_{\min}^{-1/2})
by Lemma
C.2
, we have

\left\|(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{\top}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\right\|=O_{p}(\psi_{\min}^{-1}).

Hence, by claim
2
, we have
||a_{1-2}||=O_{p}\left(\frac{r^{1/2}\sqrt{N}(\log N)^{\nu}}{\psi_{\min}}+\frac{Nq_{3,t}(\log N)^{\nu}}{\psi_{\min}}\right)
.

Claim 2
.

We have

\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}v_{k}^{0}\right\|=O_{p}\left(r^{1/2}\sqrt{N}(\log N)^{\nu}+Nq_{3,t}(\log N)^{\nu}\right).

Lastly, we have by Lemma
B.8
that

\displaystyle\left\|a_{1-3}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq T\left\|((\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{is}R_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq T\left\|((\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\right\|^{2}\left(\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\epsilon_{is}^{2}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|R_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N\max\{N^{2},T^{2}\}(\log N)^{\nu}}{\psi_{\min}^{4}}\right).

Therefore, we have

\left\|a_{1}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}\left(r^{1/2}\kappa^{1/2}(\log N)^{\nu/2}+\frac{Nq_{3,t}(\log N)^{\nu}}{\psi_{\min}}+\frac{\sqrt{N}\max\{N,T\}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi_{\min}^{2}}\right).

In addition, we have by Lemma
B.9
that w.h.p.,

\displaystyle\left\|a_{2}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\epsilon_{is}v_{s}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{2}\leq\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\epsilon_{is}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|v_{s}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\epsilon_{is}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|v_{s}^{0}\right\|^{2}\right).

Hence, we have
\left\|a_{2}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{p}\left(\kappa^{2}N(\log N)^{2\nu}q^{2}_{2,t}\right)
. To sum up, we have

\displaystyle\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bar{\bm{X}})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\kappa\sqrt{N}(\log N)^{\nu}q_{2,t}+r^{1/2}k^{1/2}(\log N)^{\nu/2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.+\frac{Nq_{3,t}(\log N)^{\nu}}{\psi_{\min}}+\frac{\sqrt{N}\max\{N,T\}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi_{\min}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}\mathbf{O}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\mathbf{R}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{N}(\log N)^{\nu}q_{2,t}}{\psi^{1/2}_{\min}}+\frac{r^{1/2}\kappa^{3/2}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi^{1/2}_{\min}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.+\frac{\kappa Nq_{3,t}(\log N)^{\nu}}{\psi^{3/2}_{\min}}+\frac{\kappa\sqrt{N}\max\{N,T\}(\log N)^{\nu/2}}{\psi_{\min}^{5/2}}\right).

(iv) This proof is the same as that of Lemma
B.3
(iv). Hence, we omit it.
\square

Proof of Claim
2
.

Note that

\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}v_{k}^{0}\right\|\lesssim\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}(\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}])v_{k}^{0}\right\|+\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}]v_{k}^{0}\right\|.

We bound the first term. By the independence of error terms across
i
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}(\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}])v_{k}^{0}\right\|^{2}\right|\bm{M}^{0}\right]=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}(\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}])v_{k}^{0}\right\|^{2}\right|\bm{M}^{0}\right].

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}(\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}])v_{k}^{0}\right\|^{2}\right|\bm{M}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{r}\left|\mathbf{V}^{0(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}_{r,l}\text{Cov}\left(\epsilon_{is}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)\mathbf{V}^{0(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r,l}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{r}\left\|\mathbf{V}^{0(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{r,l}\right\|^{2}\left\|\text{Cov}\left(\epsilon_{is}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\text{Cov}\left(\epsilon_{is}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)\right\|\left\|\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim r,

where
\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
is the matrix derived by zeroing out the columns in
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)
from
\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0}
and
\epsilon_{is}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
is the matrix derived by zeroing out the columns in
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)
from
\epsilon_{is}\mathbf{e}_{i}=[\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{i1},\cdots,\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{iT}]^{\top}
. Here, we use Assumption B’(iii) to have

\left\|\text{Cov}\left(\epsilon_{is}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)\right\|_{1}=\max_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}|\text{Cov}(\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik},\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{il})|\leq C

for some constant
C>0
where
s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)
. Hence, we have

\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}(\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}])v_{k}^{0}\right\|=O_{p}(r^{1/2}\sqrt{N}(\log N)^{\nu}).

In addition, by Assumption E’(ii), we have

\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)^{c}}\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{is}\epsilon_{ik}]v_{k}^{0}\right\|=O_{p}\left(Nq_{3,t}(\log N)^{\nu}\right).\ \ \square

Lemma B.8
.

Let
(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))},\widehat{\bm{F}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})
be the PC estimators derived from
\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\bm{M}^{0}+\bm{E}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
where
\bm{E}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
is the matrix which replaces the columns in
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)
of
\bm{E}
with zeros. Then, we have the following decomposition:

\displaystyle u_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{-1}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}u_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{\top}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0\top}\mathbf{e}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{i}+T^{1/2}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))},

(10)

where
\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}=\bm{\Lambda}^{0\top}\bm{\Lambda}^{0}\bm{F}^{0\top}\widehat{\bm{F}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-2}
and
\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\left(\widehat{\bm{F}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}\bm{F}^{0}/T\right)^{-1}
. In addition,

\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\mathcal{R}_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\max\{N,T\}}{\min\{N,T\}\psi_{\min}^{2}}\right).

Proof of Lemma
B.8
.

By the equation (15) in
Bai and Ng, 2023
, we have the following expansion:

\displaystyle\widehat{\lambda}_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{-1}\lambda_{i}^{0}+\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{\top}\bm{F}^{0\top}\mathbf{e}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{i}/T+\mathcal{R}_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))},

where

\mathcal{R}_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=\left(\widehat{\bm{F}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bm{F}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{e}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{i}/T.

Then, using the relations

\widehat{\lambda}_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}=T^{-1/2}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}u_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))},\ \ \lambda_{i}^{0}=T^{-1/2}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{-1}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}u_{i}^{0},\ \ \bm{F}^{0}=T^{1/2}\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\mathbf{H}^{0},

we have

\displaystyle T^{-1/2}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}u_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/2}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{-1}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}u_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle\ \ +T^{-1/2}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{\top}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0\top}\mathbf{e}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{i}+\mathcal{R}_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))},

and

\displaystyle u_{i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{-1}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}u_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{\top}(\mathbf{H}^{0})^{\top}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{0})^{1/2}\mathbf{V}_{r}^{0\top}\mathbf{e}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}_{i}+T^{1/2}(\mathbf{D}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}.

We can easily check that
\left\|\left(\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,1}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right)^{-1}\right\|,\left\|\mathbf{H}_{\rm BN,0}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
. In addition, by Proposition 2 of
Bai and Ng, 2023
, we have

\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\mathcal{R}_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\max\{N,T\}}{\min\{N,T\}N^{\alpha}T}\right).

Moreover, as noted in Section
C.1
,
N^{\alpha}T\asymp\psi_{\min}^{2}
with probability converging to 1. Hence, we can say

\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\mathcal{R}_{\lambda,i}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\max\{N,T\}}{\min\{N,T\}\psi_{\min}^{2}}\right).\ \ \square

Lemma B.9
.

When
\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|v_{s}^{0}\right\|^{2}\ll 1/\kappa^{2}
and
\left\|\bm{E}\right\|\ll\psi_{\min}
, we have
||v_{s}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}||\lesssim\kappa||v_{s}^{0}||
for all
s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)
.

Proof of Lemma
B.9
.

Fix
s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)
. Note that
\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}-\bm{M}^{0}=\bm{E}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
where
\bm{E}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
replaces the columns in
\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)
of
\bm{E}
with zeros. Denote the matrix derived by zeroing out the
s
-th column of
\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}
by
\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}
and

\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}=\begin{bmatrix}0&\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}\\
\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero\top}&0\end{bmatrix}.

In addition, denote the corresponding leading
r
eigenvectors of
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}
by
\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}_{r}\\
\mathbf{V}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}_{r}\end{bmatrix}
. Here,
\mathbf{U}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}_{r}\mathbf{D}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}_{r}\mathbf{V}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero\top}_{r}
is the top-r singular value decomposition of
\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}
.

First, we confirm that
||v_{s}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}||=0
. Note that entries on
(N+s)
-th row of
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}
is zeros. So, if there is an eigenvector whose entries on
(N+s)
-th row is not zero, the corresponding eigenvalue must be zero. However, the top-
r
eigenvalues of
\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}
are bigger than
\frac{1}{2}\psi_{\min}
by Weyl’s theorem because

\left\|\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}-\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}\right\|=\left\|\bm{X}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}-\bm{M}^{0}\right\|\leq\left\|\bm{M}^{0}_{\cdot,s}\right\|+\left\|\bm{E}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|<\frac{1}{4}\psi_{\min}.

It follows from
\left\|\bm{M}^{0}_{\cdot,s}\right\|=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}(m_{is}^{0})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\leq\kappa\psi_{\min}\left\|v_{s}^{0}\right\|<\frac{1}{8}\psi_{\min}
by the assumption
\sum_{s\in\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)}\left\|v_{s}^{0}\right\|^{2}\ll 1/\kappa^{2}
,
and
\left\|\bm{E}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|\leq\left\|\bm{E}\right\|<\frac{1}{8}\psi_{\min}
. Then, since
\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}
is the collection of top-
r
eigenvectors, we have
W^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}_{N+s}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}v^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}_{s}=0
.

Next, we bound
||\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}||_{F}
. By Davis-Kahan theorem, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\ \ \leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{\psi_{\min}}\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}-\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}\right\|_{F}.

For
l\neq N+s
, we have

(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}-\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})_{l,\cdot}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}=(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}-\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})_{l,N+s}W^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}_{N+s}=0

because
W^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}_{N+s}=0
. So, we have

\displaystyle\left\|(\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}-\bar{\bm{X}}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))})_{N+s,\cdot}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}_{N+s,\cdot}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\bar{\bm{M}}^{0}_{N+s,\cdot}\right\|_{2}=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}(m_{is}^{0})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\leq\kappa\psi_{\min}\left\|v_{s}^{0}\right\|

and
||\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}||_{F}\leq 2\sqrt{2}\kappa||v_{s}^{0}||
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|v_{s}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\left\|e_{N+s}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\left\|e_{N+s}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\left\|e_{N+s}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{2}\left\|e_{N+s}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))}sgn(\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))\top}\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero})-\mathbf{W}_{r}^{(-\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t)),zero}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\left\|v_{s}^{0}\right\|

where
e_{k}
is the
k
-th column of the
(N+T)\times(N+T)
identity matrix
I_{(N+T)}
.
\square

Lemma B.10
.

\left\|\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{t}|(\mathbf{e}_{s})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}})\right\|=O_{p}\left(N^{1/3}\right)
.

Proof of Lemma
B.10
.

Let
i\neq j
. Then, by the cross-sectional independence, we have

\text{Cov}(\epsilon_{it},\epsilon_{jt}|(\mathbf{e}_{s})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}})=\text{Cov}(\epsilon_{it},\epsilon_{jt}|\{(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\}).

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}\epsilon_{jt}|\{(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}\epsilon_{jt}|\{(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},\mathbf{e}_{j}\}\right]\right|\{(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\epsilon_{jt}\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\right]\right|\{(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{jt}|(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\right].

Then, we have

\displaystyle\text{Cov}(\epsilon_{it},\epsilon_{jt}|\{(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\epsilon_{it}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}|\{(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]\right)\left(\epsilon_{jt}-\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{jt}|\{(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]\right)|\{(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}\epsilon_{jt}|\{(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}},(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{jt}|(\epsilon_{js})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\right]

\displaystyle=0.

Hence, we have

\left\|\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{t}|(\mathbf{e}_{s})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}})\right\|=\max_{1\leq i\leq N}\text{Var}(\epsilon_{it}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}})\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq N}\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}^{2}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}].

Let
w_{it}=\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{it}^{2}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}]
. By Jensen’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[w_{it}^{3}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}^{2}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\right]\right)^{3}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}^{6}|(\epsilon_{is})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}}\right]\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}^{6}\right].

Then, because for
a>0
,

P(w_{it}\geq a)\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[w_{it}^{3}\right]}{a^{3}}\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}^{6}\right]}{a^{3}},

we have for any
l_{NT}\rightarrow\infty
,

P\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq N}w_{it}\geq N^{1/3}l_{NT}\right)\leq\sum_{1\leq i\leq N}P(w_{it}\geq N^{1/3}l_{NT})\leq\frac{\max_{1\leq i\leq N}\mathbb{E}\left[\epsilon_{it}^{6}\right]}{l_{NT}^{3}}\rightarrow 0.

Therefore, we have
\max_{1\leq i\leq N}w_{it}=O_{p}(N^{1/3})
and so,
\left\|\text{Cov}(\mathbf{e}_{t}|(\mathbf{e}_{s})_{s\in(\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(t))^{c}})\right\|=O_{p}(N^{1/3})
.
\square

).
